src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6a6b7e36c7e8274794581e32%2F6a70b59f9f2ddabd1e7c04bb_4-steps_4_webm.webm" data-wf-ignore="true"></video></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="w-embed w-script"><script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/gsap@3.14.1/dist/gsap.min.js"></script>
<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const steps = document.querySelectorAll('.step_card');
  const visualItems = document.querySelectorAll('.visual_item');
  const stepDuration = 4000; // 4 seconds
  let currentStep = 0;
  let ticker;
  let progressTween;
  let hasStarted = false; // NEW ΓÇö prevents it from restarting every time it scrolls back into view

  function getVideo(visual) {
    return visual ? visual.querySelector('video') : null;
  }
  function stopVideo(visual) {
    const video = getVideo(visual);
    if (!video) return;
    video.pause();
    video.currentTime = 0;
  }
  function startVideo(visual) {
    const video = getVideo(visual);
    if (!video) return;
    video.currentTime = 0;
    const playPromise = video.play();
    if (playPromise !== undefined) {
      playPromise.catch(() => {});
    }
  }
  function goToStep(index) {
    clearInterval(ticker);
    if (progressTween) progressTween.kill();
    steps.forEach(s => {
      const fill = s.querySelector('[data-progress-fill]');
      gsap.set(fill, { width: '0%' });
      s.classList.remove('is-active');
    });
    visualItems.forEach(v => {
      v.classList.remove('is-active');
      stopVideo(v);
    });
    currentStep = index;
    const activeStep = steps[currentStep];
    const activeVisual = visualItems[currentStep];
    activeStep.classList.add('is-active');
    activeVisual.classList.add('is-active');
    startVideo(activeVisual);
    const activeFill = activeStep.querySelector('[data-progress-fill]');
    progressTween = gsap.fromTo(activeFill,
      { width: '0%' },
      {
        width: '100%',
        duration: stepDuration / 1000,
        ease: 'none',
        onComplete: () => {
          let next = (currentStep + 1) % steps.length;
          goToStep(next);
        }
      }
    );
  }
  steps.forEach((step, index) => {
    step.addEventListener('click', () => {
      goToStep(index);
    });
  });

  // NEW ΓÇö only start once the section scrolls into view
  const sectionEl = document.querySelector('.section_4-steps'); // ≡ƒæê replace with your actual section's class or ID
  if (sectionEl) {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      entries.forEach(entry => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting && !hasStarted) {
          hasStarted = true;
          goToStep(0);
          observer.unobserve(sectionEl);
        }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.3 }); // fires once 30% of the section is visible ΓÇö tweak as needed

    observer.observe(sectionEl);
  } else {
    // fallback: if the section selector isn't found, just start immediately
    goToStep(0);
  }
});
</script></div><div class="w-embed"><style>

class="what-brisk-does_content"><div class="header_wrapper align-center"><div class="margin-bottom margin-small"><p class="heading-eyebrow text-align-center">What Brisk Does</p></div><h2 class="heading-h3 max-width-860">Start every day ready</h2><div class="margin-top margin-small"><p class="text-align-center text-size-20 max-width-800">Brisk brings AI into the tools you already teach with. From planning to feedback to student support, it covers the full teaching and learning cycle.</p></div></div><div class="what-brisk-does_tools"><div class="w-embed w-script"><script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const toolItems = document.querySelectorAll('.tool-item');
  const mediaImages = document.querySelectorAll('.tool-media_image');
  const triggers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-tool-trigger]');

  // NEW ΓÇö grabs the <video> tag living inside a .tool-media_image wrapper
  function getVideo(media) {
    return media ? media.querySelector('video') : null;
  }

  // NEW ΓÇö pauses + rewinds a video so it's not silently playing/looping while hidden
  function stopVideo(media) {
    const video = getVideo(media);
    if (!video) return;
    video.pause();
    video.currentTime = 0;
  }

  // NEW ΓÇö rewinds AND plays the newly active item's video
  function startVideo(media) {
    const video = getVideo(media);
    if (!video) return;
    video.currentTime = 0;
    const playPromise = video.play();
    if (playPromise !== undefined) {
      playPromise.catch(() => {}); // ignore ΓÇö safe since it's muted
    }
  }

  triggers.forEach((trigger, index) => {
    trigger.addEventListener('click', () => {
      // 1. Update active state for content items
      toolItems.forEach(item => item.classList.remove('is-active'));
      toolItems[index].classList.add('is-active');

      // 2. Update active image with a slight delay for smooth transition
      mediaImages.forEach(img => {
        img.classList.remove('is-active');
        stopVideo(img); // NEW ΓÇö pause + rewind every video whenever the selection changes, not just the outgoing one
      });
      mediaImages[index].classList.add('is-active');
      startVideo(mediaImages[index]); // NEW ΓÇö force this item's video to start from frame 0
    });
  });

  // NEW ΓÇö whichever item is marked active by default in your markup (looks like "Create Content"
  // per the screenshot) won't get a click event, so its video needs to be kicked off manually here.
  // Without this, the very first video just sits frozen until someone clicks something.
  const initialIndex = Array.from(mediaImages).findIndex(m => m.classList.contains('is-active'));
  if (initialIndex !== -1) {
    startVideo(mediaImages[initialIndex]);
  }
});
</script></div><div class="w-embed"><style>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const cards = document.querySelectorAll('[data-testimonial-card]');
  const allVideos = document.querySelectorAll('[data-video]');

  cards.forEach(card => {
    const video = card.querySelector('[data-video]');
    const trigger = card.querySelector('[data-video-trigger]');

    const togglePlay = () => {
      if (video.paused) {
        // Stop all other videos first
        allVideos.forEach(v => {
          if (v !== video) {
            v.pause();
            v.closest('[data-testimonial-card]').classList.remove('is-playing');
          }
        });
        
        video.play();
        card.classList.add('is-playing');
      } else {
        video.pause();
        card.classList.remove('is-playing');
      }
    };

    trigger.addEventListener('click', togglePlay);
  });
});
</script></div><div class="w-embed"><style>
[data-carousel-container] {
  --site-max-width: 1200px;
  --site-padding: 2rem;
  --content-width: min(var(--site-max-width), 100vw - var(--site-padding) * 2);
  --carousel-gutter: calc((100vw - var(--content-width)) / 2);
}

.testimonial_card.is-playing .testimonial_play-icon {
  display: none;
}

.testimonial_card.is-playing .testimonial_pause-icon {
  display: block;
}

.testimonial_card.is-playing .testimonial_overlay {
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
}

@media (max-width: 991px) {
  .testimonials_list::-webkit-scrollbar {
    display: none;
  }
}

  .slider-tools-icons_content p {
  display: -webkit-box;
  -webkit-line-clamp: 3;
  -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
  overflow: hidden;
}

<script>
  window.FinsweetAttributes ||= [];
  window.FinsweetAttributes.push([
    'list',
    (listInstances) => {
      const [listInstance] = listInstances;
      if (!listInstance) return;

      const mask = document.querySelector('.swiper-wrapper');
      if (!mask) return;

      const equalizeSlideHeights = () => {
        // Release any min-height we previously set, so we can measure true content height
        mask.style.minHeight = '0px';
        mask.style.height = 'auto';

        const cards = mask.querySelectorAll('.slider-tools-images_content');
        let tallest = 0;

        cards.forEach((card) => {
          card.style.height = 'auto'
          const height = card.getBoundingClientRect().height;
          // if (card.scrollHeight > height) tallest = card.scrollHeight;
          if (height > tallest) tallest = height;
        });

        // .w-slide, .height-100, and the card itself are all height:100% already ΓÇö
        // setting min-height on the mask cascades down to all of them.
        if (tallest > 0) {
          mask.style.minHeight = `${Math.ceil(tallest)}px`;
          mask.style.height = `${Math.ceil(tallest)}px`;
        }
      };

      const runAfterPaint = () => requestAnimationFrame(equalizeSlideHeights);

      // Initial run, once Finsweet has injected the CMS items into the slides
      runAfterPaint();

      // Re-run any time Finsweet re-renders items (filter, sort, load more, pagination)
      listInstance.addHook('afterRender', (items) => {
        runAfterPaint();
        return items;
      });

      // Re-run on resize/breakpoint changes ΓÇö card text can reflow to more or fewer lines
      let resizeTimeout;
      window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
        clearTimeout(resizeTimeout);
        resizeTimeout = setTimeout(equalizeSlideHeights, 150);
      });

      // Re-run once lazy-loaded icon images finish loading, since they can grow a card after first paint
      mask.querySelectorAll('img').forEach((img) => {
        if (!img.complete) {
          img.addEventListener('load', equalizeSlideHeights, { once: true });
        }
      });
    },
  ]);
</script></div><div class="sliderflow-bundle"><div class="sliderflow-container"><div class="swiper-container"><div swipershowbulletsdesktop="default" swipergrabcursortablet="default" document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {

  const tabLinks = document.querySelectorAll("[tab-content-data]");
  const panes = document.querySelectorAll("[tab-pane-data]");
  const section = document.querySelector(".home_tools-tab");

  function stopAllVideos() {
    document.querySelectorAll("[tab-pane-data] iframe").forEach(iframe => {
      const src = iframe.src;
      iframe.src = "";
      iframe.src = src.replace("ap=true", "ap=false");
    });
  }

  function playVideoInPane(pane) {
    const iframe = pane.querySelector("iframe");
    if (!iframe) return;

    let src = iframe.src;

    if (!src.includes("ap=true")) {
      src = src.replace("ap=false", "ap=true");
    }

    iframe.src = src;
  }

  function activateTab(link) {
    const key = link.getAttribute("tab-content-data");

    tabLinks.forEach(l => l.classList.remove("is-active"));
    panes.forEach(p => p.classList.remove("is-active"));

    link.classList.add("is-active");

    const targetPane = document.querySelector(`[tab-pane-data="${key}"]`);
    if (!targetPane) return;

    targetPane.classList.add("is-active");

    stopAllVideos();
    playVideoInPane(targetPane);
  }

  tabLinks.forEach(link => {
    link.addEventListener("click", () => activateTab(link));
  });

  if (section && tabLinks.length) {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries, obs) => {
      if (entries[0].isIntersecting) {
        activateTab(tabLinks[0]);
        obs.disconnect();
      }
    }, { threshold: 0.5 });

    observer.observe(section);
  }

});
</script>
<script>
(function initializeDeferredVideos() {
  /*
   * Deferred vidzflow loader:
   * - Looks for <deferred-video> placeholders once the DOM is ready
   * - Copies their attributes to a real <iframe>
   * - Applies defaults for missing attributes/styles
   * - Replaces placeholders so the iframe loads only after DOM load
   *
   * Why?
   * vidzflow videos can be over 50mb and will cause "infinite loading" states that can cause problems
   * with the CookieYes scans. Also, we shouldn't block the critical render path with resources that large
   */
  const PLACEHOLDER_TAG = "deferred-video";

  const copyAttributes = (source, target) => {
    Array.from(source.attributes).forEach(attribute => {
      target.setAttribute(attribute.name, attribute.value);
    });
  };

  const buildIframeFromPlaceholder = placeholder => {
    const src = placeholder.getAttribute("src");

    if (!src) {
      return null;
    }

    const iframe = document.createElement("iframe");
    copyAttributes(placeholder, iframe);
    return iframe;
  };

  const renderDeferredEmbeds = () => {
    const placeholders = document.querySelectorAll(PLACEHOLDER_TAG);

    Array.from(placeholders).forEach(placeholder => {
      const iframe = buildIframeFromPlaceholder(placeholder);

      if (!iframe) {
        return;
      }

      placeholder.replaceWith(iframe);
    });
  };

  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", renderDeferredEmbeds);
  } else {
    renderDeferredEmbeds();
  }
})();
</script></div></div><div style="inset: 0px; position: fixed; z-index: -1;"><iframe class="chilipiper-frame" src="https://briskteaching.chilipiper.com/concierge-router/copy-of-demo-request---hubspot-form-march-16-2026?trigger=ThirdPartyForm&amp;timezone=UTC&amp;sourceUrl=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.briskteaching.com%2F" style="border: 0px; overflow: hidden; height: 0px; width: 1px; min-width: 0px;"></iframe></div><script src="https://d3e54v103j8qbb.cloudfront.net/js/jquery-3.5.1.min.dc5e7f18c8.js?site=6a6b7e36c7e8274794581e32" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha256-9/aliU8dGd2tb6OSsuzixeV4y/faTqgFtohetphbbj0=" crossorigin="anonymous"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6a6b7e36c7e8274794581e32/js/webflow.schunk.f2efb3c5440a81cf.js" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha384-VK02VJvMib7ytrF5M2joPWNtDQs366FZgEQ5B+Iwnjx0WBnwQXfootyziSYxdrmS" crossorigin="anonymous"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6a6b7e36c7e8274794581e32/js/webflow.schunk.970d9d45a9f93b92.js" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha384-BqJBCaal9cYpCyr21IFGmPR/puvcDyB5tJOgYzTLkjCKamAU2GNE2giM96XyLDPy" crossorigin="anonymous"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6a6b7e36c7e8274794581e32/js/webflow.777efe86.036f26d7dcbd5aaa.js" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha384-L38LY3Accdl1BGgtrmZshOVcoVRXoDCneiyrb7Xx0vVlZcTRvipDi+uOkoJteZ+O" crossorigin="anonymous"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/gsap/3.15.0/gsap.min.js" type="text/javascript"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/gsap/3.15.0/ScrollTrigger.min.js" type="text/javascript"></script><script type="text/javascript">gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);</script><!-- Google Tag Manager (noscript) -->

<script type="text/javascript">
    /* apply only to forms with the action pointing to Loops */
    $('form[action^="https://app.loops.so"]').each(function (i, el) {
        form = $(el);
        form.submit(function (e) {
          /* stop the form from submitting */
          e.preventDefault();
          form = $(e.target);
          action = form.attr('action');
          /* submit the form via ajax */
          $.ajax({
            url: action,
            method: "POST",
            data: form.serialize(),
            dataType: "json",
            success: function (data) {
              if (data.success) {
                /* successful submission - hide the form and show the success message */
                parent = $(form.parent());
                parent.children('form').css('display', 'none');
                parent.children('.w-form-done').css('display', 'block');
              } else {
                parent = $(form.parent());
                parent.find('.w-form-fail').css('display', 'block');
              }
            },
            error: function () {
              /* failed submission - show the failure message */
              parent = $(form.parent());
              parent.find('.w-form-fail').css('display', 'block');
            }
          });
        });
    });
</script>
